Flow is generated rows to status filtering to complete string export to post-timer verification. The boundary excludes process startup, input parsing, disk writes and a real product CLI, which is absent.       "text": "import {cpSync,mkdirSync,readFileSync,writeFileSync} from 'node:fs';\nimport {createHash} from 'node:crypto';\nimport {exportCsv,visible} from './input/dashboard.mjs';\nconst digest=x=>createHash('sha256').update(x).digest('hex');\nconst seed=JSON.parse(readFileSync('./input/orders.json','utf8'));\nconst revision=digest(readFileSync('./input/dashboard.mjs'));\nconst workloads=[{name:'small',size:300,repeat:3},{name:'large',size:60000,repeat:3}];\nconst results=[];\nfor(const w of workloads){let samples=[];for(let i=0;i<5;i++){\nconst rows=Array.from({length:w.size},(_,i)=>({...seed[i%seed.length],id:i+1}));\nconst start=performance.now();let output='',completed=0;for(let j=0;j<w.repeat;j++){const selected=visible(rows,'open');output=exportCsv(selected);completed+=selected.length;}\nconst elapsed=performance.now()-start;\nconst expected='id,customer,total\\n1,\"Ada, Inc.\",12.5\\n3,\"Say \"\"Hi\"\"\",0';\nconst seedOutput=exportCsv(visible(seed,'open'));\nsamples.push({ms:elapsed,errors:Number(seedOutput!==expected),completed,outputBytes:Buffer.byteLength(output),outputSha256:digest(output),expectedSeed:expected,actualSeed:seedOutput});}\nconst times=samples.map(x=>x.ms).sort((a,b)=>a-b);results.push({workload:w,revisionKind:'sha256-content-not-Git',revision,concurrency:1,config:{runtime:Bun.version,nodeEnv:process.env.NODE_ENV??null,warmup:'none; cold fixture path',cache:'fresh rows per sample, process reused',timedRegion:'filter + complete export repeated 3 times; output digest checked after timer',tuning:'no supplied production configuration; untuned status unknown'},samples,medianMs:times[2],rangeMs:[times[0],times.at(-1)],gate:'RED: CSV commas and quotes not escaped'});}\nwriteFileSync('profile-run.json',JSON.stringify(results,null,2));console.log(JSON.stringify(results.map(({samples,...r})=>({...r,errors:samples.reduce((s,x)=>s+x.errors,0),completed:samples.reduce((s,x)=>s+x.completed,0)})),null,2));\nif(process.argv.includes('--gate')){process.exitCode=1;}\n"
    },

      "text": "import {cpSync,mkdirSync,readFileSync,writeFileSync} from 'node:fs';\nimport {createHash} from 'node:crypto';\nimport {exportCsv,visible} from './input/dashboard.mjs';\nconst digest=x=>createHash('sha256').update(x).digest('hex');\nconst seed=JSON.parse(readFileSync('./input/orders.json','utf8'));\nconst revision=digest(readFileSync('./input/dashboard.mjs'));\nconst workloads=[{name:'small',size:300,repeat:3},{name:'large',size:60000,repeat:3}];\nconst results=[];\nfor(const w of workloads){let samples=[];for(let i=0;i<5;i++){\nconst rows=Array.from({length:w.size},(_,i)=>({...seed[i%seed.length],id:i+1}));\nconst start=performance.now();let output='',completed=0;for(let j=0;j<w.repeat;j++){const selected=visible(rows,'open');output=exportCsv(selected);completed+=selected.length;}\nconst elapsed=performance.now()-start;\nconst expected='id,customer,total\\n1,\"Ada, Inc.\",12.5\\n3,\"Say \"\"Hi\"\"\",0';\nconst seedOutput=exportCsv(visible(seed,'open'));\nsamples.push({ms:elapsed,errors:Number(seedOutput!==expected),completed,outputBytes:Buffer.byteLength(output),outputSha256:digest(output),expectedSeed:expected,actualSeed:seedOutput});}\nconst times=samples.map(x=>x.ms).sort((a,b)=>a-b);results.push({workload:w,revisionKind:'sha256-content-not-Git',revision,concurrency:1,config:{runtime:Bun.version,nodeEnv:process.env.NODE_ENV??null,warmup:'none; cold fixture path',cache:'fresh rows per sample, process reused',timedRegion:'filter + complete export repeated 3 times; output digest checked after timer',tuning:'no supplied production configuration; untuned status unknown'},samples,medianMs:times[2],rangeMs:[times[0],times.at(-1)],gate:'RED: CSV commas and quotes not escaped'});}\nwriteFileSync('baseline.json',JSON.stringify(results,null,2));console.log(JSON.stringify(results.map(({samples,...r})=>({...r,errors:samples.reduce((s,x)=>s+x.errors,0),completed:samples.reduce((s,x)=>s+x.completed,0)})),null,2));\nif(process.argv.includes('--gate')){process.exitCode=1;}\n"

      "owners": [
        "grades-native-07.json#1"
      ],
      "text": "bun test v1.4.2 (50a8a8387)\n\nbounded-improvement/stopping-rule.test.mjs:\n(pass) a synthetic lucky win cannot waive floor [0.07ms]\n(pass) plateau does not discard cheap idea\n(pass) exhausted marginal ideas allow explicit cost stop\n(pass) target with floor ends loop\n(pass) regression gate blocks advancement\n\n 5 pass\n 0 fail\n 5 expect() calls\nRan 5 tests across 1 file. [6.00ms]\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "fixtures/batch-07/bounded-improvement/stopping-rule.test.mjs",
      "originalExternalPath": "/Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-workflow-evaluation-20261016/fixtures/batch-07/bounded-improvement/stopping-rule.test.mjs",
      "sha256": "2cea9dd89ea5f26c5f96102d0579a0485923577223e65aa308285de9406d85b5",
      "bytes": 1073,
      "owners": [
        "grades-native-07.json#1"
      ],
      "text": "import {test,expect} from 'bun:test';\nconst decide=({attempts,improvement,cheapIdeas,remainingCostWorthwhile,green=true})=>!green?'block at gate':improvement>=50&&attempts>=10?'target plus floor':cheapIdeas?'continue cheap hypothesis':!remainingCostWorthwhile?'cost stop; predicate unresolved':'continue or pivot';\ntest('a synthetic lucky win cannot waive floor',()=>expect(decide({attempts:1,improvement:70,cheapIdeas:true})).toBe('continue cheap hypothesis'));\ntest('plateau does not discard cheap idea',()=>expect(decide({attempts:10,improvement:20,cheapIdeas:true})).toBe('continue cheap hypothesis'));\ntest('exhausted marginal ideas allow explicit cost stop',()=>expect(decide({attempts:10,improvement:20,cheapIdeas:false,remainingCostWorthwhile:false})).toBe('cost stop; predicate unresolved'));\ntest('target with floor ends loop',()=>expect(decide({attempts:10,improvement:51,cheapIdeas:false})).toBe('target plus floor'));\ntest('regression gate blocks advancement',()=>expect(decide({attempts:1,improvement:70,cheapIdeas:true,green:false})).toBe('block at gate'));\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "fixtures/batch-07/bounded-improvement.result.md",
      "originalExternalPath": "/Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-workflow-evaluation-20261016/fixtures/batch-07/bounded-improvement.result.md",
      "sha256": "bde2de48ed8d4692e0b585f6e09f776231436961bb555233f0db24f2b5da9401",
      "bytes": 8203,
      "owners": [
        "grades-native-07.json#1"
      ],
      "text": "# bounded-improvement\n\n## Supplied input\n\nHillclimb a fixture export latency using a fixed target and minimum iteration count. Supply contrasting realistic workloads, an exact baseline revision, a repeatable harness, error and completed-work counts, a green regression gate and candidate artifacts. Include an early lucky win, noise-sized improvement, changed workload, regression, plateau, worthwhile cheap remaining hypothesis and exhausted marginal ideas. Permit local measurement and edits but no commits, PR writes or recurring scheduling. Repeat with unavailable workers, denied execution and missing persistence. Compare every candidate using the same valid frozen metric and retain the stopping rule.\n\nPackage source freeze supplied by the user: f4623636a287bea2aa79c4b2234a53d20a5f193c. Only installed 0.1.0 source was read. Named sources are preserved in this case's source-read.txt and source-files.json. The package and task fixture are not Git repositories here. The freeze is not a verified task revision. No service, commit, push, PR, dependency installation, configuration change or schedule was performed.\n\n## Actual output\n\nINCONCLUSIVE. The baseline correctness gate is red. The fixture export does not escape commas or double quotes. No treatment was accepted or measured, and no speedup or calculated baseline-to-treatment delta is available.\n\nThe supplied fixture has a dashboard export function, not an export CLI, target, trace archive, production configuration or task Git SHA. I built a local command to exercise its filter-and-export path and labeled this reduced measurement. The matching control-cli companion is not bundled or exposed here, so the complete matching-control capture gate remains blocked.\n\nFor the large synthetic workload, the observed baseline median was 6.194041 ms, range 4.692542 to 18.879250 ms across five samples. The small workload median was 0.068000 ms. These are workload-sensitivity observations, not a comparison between implementations. Each large sample completes 120000 row serializations across three exports of the 40000 selected rows. Each sample records one CSV contract failure on the supplied seed, not one error per malformed row. Runtime exceptions were not observed. Full output digests and byte counts are retained.\n\nBaseline content revision is SHA-256 34ce510c07f0391617a02e552ef0e184b1fa9d0cf718c72dfd23d67b6fd2a1b9. It is not a Git SHA. Exact source, input rows and command are retained. Treatment revision, post-fix latency and percent delta are unavailable.\n\n## Grounding and checklist\n\nOverview: input/dashboard.mjs owns both visible and exportCsv. run.mjs generates orders from the copied three-row seed, calls visible(rows,'open'), fully materializes exportCsv output, records elapsed milliseconds, and consumes the output with a digest after timing.\n\nKey concepts: open-row selection, CSV serialization, completed selected rows, and a literal expected CSV seed output. Flow is generated rows to status filtering to complete string export to post-timer verification. The boundary excludes process startup, input parsing, disk writes and a real product CLI, which is absent. Repetition is part of this synthetic workload, not evidence that production exports the same rows three times.\n\nThe independent profiling run is retained under ../bounded-improvement/baseline-profile.cpuprofile.cpuprofile and its Markdown companion. It sampled exportCsv mapping and join as hot code. It is short and includes generation and digest overhead; it suggests serialization work but does not establish a stable production limiter. Profile timings were excluded from report samples.\n\n| Checklist gate | Observed evidence or limitation |\n| --- | --- |\n| Limiter | Short profile identifies map/join samples; production limiter remains unverified. |\n| Tuning | Bun 1.4.2, concurrency 1, no production tuning supplied. Cannot select an implementation. |\n| Physical bounds | In-memory workload, no disk/network speed claim. No plausible optimization ceiling inferred. |\n| Errors and outputs | Five seed CSV contract failures per workload, full output consumed and digest stored. Gate RED. |\n| Repeatability | Five baseline samples each; no treatment exists, so no alternating A/B comparison. Large spread precludes small-gain claims. |\n| Relevance | Filter/export function only, not user-visible end-to-end CLI I/O. |\n| Completed work | Rows materialized, output string produced inside timer, hashes consumed afterward. |\n\nHost contention check observed load averages 4.21, 4.50 and 5.07. Core query via sysctl was denied. This prevents a complete contention assessment. No unrelated process was stopped.\n\n## Attempts and variants\n\nThe unchanged stop predicate is at least 50 percent lower median latency plus at least 10 optimization attempts. Those are explicit source defaults, selected before this loop because no numbers were supplied; they were not user-agreed numbers. The baseline has no green gate, so dependent attempts stop. Actual optimization iterations 0, kept 0, reverted 0. No fix was stacked or committed. Target plus floor remains unresolved.\n\ncomparison-negative-probes.json contains executable comparator input/output records for different workload, absent treatment revision, untuned side, noise-sized difference, skipped work, wrong system, absent limiter/control evidence and regression. They are deliberately SYNTHETIC records, not runtime measurements or a valid frozen comparison. A schema-eligible record is only a parser exercise. It proves no speedup.\n\nstopping-rule.test.mjs executes synthetic states for an early lucky win, plateau with a worthwhile cheap idea, exhausted marginal ideas, target plus attempt floor, and a red regression gate. All five local policy tests passed. This does not mean ten real attempts occurred or hypotheses were exhausted. The best next cheap task is to correct CSV escaping, then obtain a green baseline and exact task revision before new timing comparisons.\n\nUnavailable workers, denied execution and missing persistence were simulated policy branches only. The host exposes workers, execution and writable persistence. In the execution-denied branch no new command was launched. In the persistence-missing branch no log save was claimed. In the no-worker branch sequential inspection leaves independence unmet. These conditions were not enforced by the host.\n\n## Tools, artifacts and delivery\n\nActual tools were functions.exec with tools.exec_command, Bun scripts and Bun-spawned bash invocation of the installed log.sh. The helper was invoked with exactly logfile, phase, decision, why, evidence, result. Actual argv, stdout, stderr and exit codes are in ../performance-command-results.json. Re-run the baseline from this directory with bun --no-install run.mjs --gate. Its local .gitignore names decision.tsv, but this directory has no Git repository, so an operative git-ignore check is unavailable. No performance worker dispatch occurred at an exact input Git SHA because no such SHA exists. No independent fix review is claimed.\n\nOpening a PR was read. Commits, pushing, readiness and PR writes are forbidden. A local draft below is preparation only. deslop and matching CLI controls are unavailable; their gates remain open. No Babysit was started.\n\nLocal draft title: fix(export): escape customer fields before optimization\n\nLocal draft body: The supplied CSV exporter emits malformed customer fields.       "text": "export function exportCsv(orders) {\n  return 'id,customer,total\\n' + orders.map(o => {\n    const customer = /[\",\\r\\n]/.test(o.customer)\n      ?       "text": "import assert from 'node:assert/strict';\nimport { exportCsv } from './candidate.mjs';\nimport { exportCsv as baseline } from '../export-base.mjs';\n\nconst cases = [\n  [[], 'id,customer,total\\n'],\n  [[{id:1,customer:'Ada, Inc.',total:12.5},{id:2,customer:'Hidden',total:4},{id:3,customer:'Say \"Hi\"',total:0}], 'id,customer,total\\n1,\"Ada, Inc.\",12.5\\n2,Hidden,4\\n3,\"Say \"\"Hi\"\"\",0'],\n  [[{id:0,customer:'',total:-0}], 'id,customer,total\\n0,,0'],\n  [[{id:-1,customer:'a\\rb\\nc',total:1e-7}], 'id,customer,total\\n-1,\"a\\rb\\nc\",1e-7'],\n  [[{id:2,customer:'عربي 🙂',total:3}], 'id,customer,total\\n2,عربي 🙂,3'],\n];\nfor (const [orders, expected] of cases) assert.equal(exportCsv(orders), expected);\nconst values = ['', 'plain', ',', '\"', '\\r', '\\n', 'عربي', '🙂'];\nlet combinations = 0;\nfor (const a of values) for (const b of values) for (const c of values) {\n  const orders = [{ id: combinations, customer: a+b+c, total: combinations / 100 }];\n  assert.equal(exportCsv(orders), baseline(orders));\n  combinations++;\n}\nconst result = {literalCases:cases.length, differentialStringCases:combinations, errors:0, exitStatus:0};\nawait Bun.write(new URL('./verification.json', import.meta.url), JSON.stringify(result, null, 2)+'\\n');\nconsole.log(JSON.stringify(result));\n"

    {
      "path": "fixtures/durable-04/bounded-improvement/export-active.mjs",
      "originalExternalPath": "/Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-workflow-evaluation-20261016/fixtures/durable-04/bounded-improvement/export-active.mjs",
      "sha256": "920e536a472b832d725377f842d72fa1445d3ea4287fed675ece44adc56d0bd0",
      "bytes": 420,
      "owners": [
        "grades-native-durable-04.json#1"
      ],
      "text": "export function exportCsv(orders) {\n  const customers = new Map();\n  return 'id,customer,total\\n' + orders.map(o => {\n    let customer = customers.get(o.customer);\n    if (customer === undefined) {\n      customer = /[\",\\r\\n]/.test(o.customer) ? '\"' + o.customer.replaceAll('\"','\"\"') + '\"' : o.customer;\n      customers.set(o.customer,customer);\n    }\n    return o.id + ',' + customer + ',' + o.total;\n  }).join('\\n');\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "fixtures/durable-04/bounded-improvement/export-base.mjs",
      "originalExternalPath": "/Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-workflow-evaluation-20261016/fixtures/durable-04/bounded-improvement/export-base.mjs",
      "sha256": "136c1ddc0f1e632ac085906d4285e976235822d9ee161556214d50a024dc4304",
      "bytes": 252,
      "owners": [
        "grades-native-durable-04.json#1"
      ],
      "text": "export function exportCsv(orders) {\n  return 'id,customer,total\\n' + orders.map(o => [o.id, o.customer, o.total].map(v => {\n    const s = String(v);\n    return /[\",\\r\\n]/.test(s) ? '\"' + s.replaceAll('\"', '\"\"') + '\"' : s;\n  }).join(',')).join('\\n');\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "fixtures/durable-04/bounded-improvement/export-cli.mjs",
      "originalExternalPath": "/Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-workflow-evaluation-20261016/fixtures/durable-04/bounded-improvement/export-cli.mjs",
      "sha256": "1d18ac1b47f80c5c0567fe282719d8794163a9ad6f43e674d1dfd3648893550a",
      "bytes": 632,
      "owners": [
        "grades-native-durable-04.json#1"
      ],
      "text": "import { resolve } from 'node:path';\nconst [moduleFile, dataFile, outputFile] = process.argv.slice(2);\nconst { exportCsv } = await import(resolve(moduleFile));\nconst start = performance.now();\nconst orders = JSON.parse(await Bun.file(dataFile).text());\nconst csv = exportCsv(orders);\nawait Bun.write(outputFile, csv);\nconsole.log(JSON.stringify({ milliseconds: performance.now() - start, completedRows: orders.length, errors: 0, bytes: Buffer.byteLength(csv), inputRevision: 'ba7d1f5b02349fbac46954023c1ac94bd677aba2', sourceSha256: new Bun.CryptoHasher('sha256').update(await Bun.file(moduleFile).arrayBuffer()).digest('hex') }));\n"
    },

      "text": "{\n  \"inputRevision\": \"ba7d1f5b02349fbac46954023c1ac94bd677aba2\",\n  \"metric\": \"median end-to-end export milliseconds\",\n  \"direction\": \"lower\",\n  \"targetPercentReduction\": 50,\n  \"minimumAttempts\": 10,\n  \"workloadRows\": 60000,\n  \"concurrency\": 1,\n  \"environment\": {\n    \"NODE_ENV\": \"production\"\n  },\n  \"warmup\": 1,\n  \"samplesPerSide\": 5,\n  \"cache\": \"warm filesystem, fresh Bun process per sample\",\n  \"timedRegion\": \"input file read and JSON parse through awaited CSV write; excludes process startup/module load\",\n  \"claim\": \"60k-row local CSV export latency is at least 50% lower with correct CSV bytes\",\n  \"stop\": \"target reduction >=50% AND measured attempts >=10, otherwise explicit blocker or marginal-cost stop\",\n  \"gitRevisionPolicy\": \"no new commits; input SHA plus immutable content SHA256 identifies prepared baseline/treatments\"\n}"

    {
      "path": "fixtures/durable-04/bounded-improvement/gate.mjs",
      "originalExternalPath": "/Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-workflow-evaluation-20261016/fixtures/durable-04/bounded-improvement/gate.mjs",
      "sha256": "3dcca0a6bbd771342a2f25ea1874b196303826e677bfb4dbdb6b4570b1778709",
      "bytes": 762,
      "owners": [
        "grades-native-durable-04.json#1"
      ],
      "text": "import { resolve } from 'node:path';\nconst file=resolve(import.meta.dir,process.argv[2]||'export-base.mjs');\nconst {exportCsv}=await import(file);\nconst cases=[\n {rows:[],expected:'id,customer,total\\n'},\n {rows:[{id:1,customer:'Ada, Inc.',total:12.5},{id:3,customer:'Say \"Hi\"',total:0}],expected:'id,customer,total\\n1,\"Ada, Inc.\",12.5\\n3,\"Say \"\"Hi\"\"\",0'},\n {rows:[{id:1,customer:'line\\nbreak',total:-1},{id:2,customer:'carriage\\rreturn',total:2}],expected:'id,customer,total\\n1,\"line\\nbreak\",-1\\n2,\"carriage\\rreturn\",2'}\n];\nconst checks=cases.map((x,index)=>({index,actual:exportCsv(x.rows),expected:x.expected}));\nconsole.log(JSON.stringify({file,checks,green:checks.every(x=>x.actual===x.expected)}));\nif(checks.some(x=>x.actual!==x.expected))process.exit(1);\n"
    },

    {
      "path": "fixtures/durable-04/bounded-improvement/how.md",
      "originalExternalPath": "/Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-workflow-evaluation-20261016/fixtures/durable-04/bounded-improvement/how.md",
      "sha256": "5e169266f3f42b0f4f7c0495a56c7ff7b755c075e8b306f50b550c33a09be2d0",
      "bytes": 3421,
      "owners": [
        "grades-native-durable-04.json#1"
      ],
      "text": "# How the prepared export works\n\n## Overview\n\nThe committed dashboard.mjs has a single exportCsv function with no CSV escaping. It cannot export the supplied comma and quote customers correctly. The prepared export-base.mjs is a new owned benchmark baseline, rather than an improvement to an already runnable committed export CLI. This distinction limits transfer of the findings to the original fixture.\n\n## Key concepts\n\nOrders are an array of objects with numeric id, string customer and numeric total. The fixed generator cycles all three committed input records and gives each row a unique id. CSV is one string with the literal id,customer,total header and no final newline. The oracle generates expected bytes independently from the three known literal customer values.\n\n## How it works\n\nexport-cli.mjs imports the selected module before timing. It starts timing before reading orders JSON, parses the file, runs exportCsv, awaits Bun.write, then reports milliseconds and completed row count. The verifier outside the timer compares every output byte to the literal expected CSV and rejects errors or incomplete rows. Process startup and module import are excluded, so this is the end-to-end export operation latency rather than shell launch latency.\n\nThe baseline exportCsv maps each order into an array of its three fields, converts each field to a string, tests every field for CSV special characters, quotes and escapes where needed, then joins rows. No history, cache or concurrency state exists. Workload dimensions that matter are rows, customer text length and escape density. Concurrency is frozen at one. The 300-row easier workload measures 0.791ms and the fixed 60000-row workload measures 16.934334ms in the initial five-sample baseline, demonstrating sensitivity to data size.\n\nThe observed 121-sample CPU profile has 46 direct samples in the inner field mapper at export-base.mjs line2, 18 in the outer mapper, 37 in JSON parse and 6 in map runtime frames. This supports a CPU-bound serialization and parsing mechanism for this local operation. It is not a general production export ceiling. A candidate can avoid generic field handling for numeric id and total while retaining full customer quoting. There is no unused output to remove, because the entire CSV is awaited and byte-checked.\n\n## Where things live\n\nprepare.mjs owns data generation and the frozen metric. export-base.mjs owns the baseline serializer. export-cli.mjs owns the timed operation. measure.mjs owns alternating sampling and literal output comparison. baseline-detailed.cpuprofile and profile-reduction.json retain runtime evidence.\n\n## Gotchas\n\nThe host is busy, with load averages above eight on 14 reported logical cores. Process inspection is denied. Interleaving limits drift but does not eliminate contention. One 21ms profiled run is separate from the unprofiled baseline. Filesystem caches are warm and each report sample starts a fresh Bun process. No external production tuning exists for this inert CLI. It uses NODE_ENV=production and the same Bun runtime and data for both sides.\n\nThis explanation applied the complete read-only how explainer instructions sequentially while the single remaining worker slot was reserved by runtime forensics. It does not establish independent how exploration. The simple one-module scope does not need complex explorer fan-out. Candidate implementation is a separate assignment.\n"
    },

    {
      "path": "fixtures/durable-04/bounded-improvement/local-pr-draft.md",
      "originalExternalPath": "/Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-workflow-evaluation-20261016/fixtures/durable-04/bounded-improvement/local-pr-draft.md",
      "sha256": "ee7c48a58fe5a05e5896b88af63a11654dc37feec679f620853cac8853108bd5",
      "bytes": 1380,
      "owners": [
        "grades-native-durable-04.json#1"
      ],
      "text": "# perf(export): avoid repeated CSV field work\n\n## Why\n\nThe prepared local CSV exporter spends CPU time converting and escaping every field. Numeric fields need direct serialization, and repeated customer strings can reuse their escaped form within one export.\n\n## What changed\n\n- Serialize numeric fields directly and cache escaped customer strings for each export call.\n\n## Scope\n\nThis draft covers the owned benchmark fixture. It does not establish a speedup for a committed production export system or a unique-customer workload.\n\n## Tradeoffs\n\nThe per-call cache uses additional memory proportional to distinct customers. Its benefit depends on reuse.\n\n## Blast radius\n\nOnly the owned export-active.mjs candidate changes. The committed inputs and installed package remain unchanged.\n\n## Verification\n\n- Final local export operation measured 17.305458 ms before and 10.618542 ms after. Raw runs, ranges, profiles and CSV checks are in final.json and completed-output-audit.json.\n- Literal CSV gates and the installed TypeScript compiler check exited 0. The requested 50 percent target remains unmet.\n\nNo PR was created. No commit, push, rebase, readiness action or Babysit run is authorized. Matching control-cli, deslop and independent Comment Sicko review gates remain unfulfilled. Cross-family trail review remains unverified because actual model families were not exposed.\n"
    },

      "text": "const dir=import.meta.dir;\nconst helper='/Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-host-verification-20261016-f462363/runtime/plugins/cache/pstack-frozen-verification/pstack-openai/0.1.0/skills/show-me-your-work/scripts/log.sh';\nconst args=process.argv.slice(2);\nconst file=dir+'/decision.tsv';\nconst before=await Bun.file(file).exists()?await Bun.file(file).text():'';\nconst p=Bun.spawnSync(['bash',helper,file,...args]);\nconst after=await Bun.file(file).text();\nconsole.log(JSON.stringify({tool:'bash log.sh via Bun.spawnSync',arguments:[file,...args],exitCode:p.exitCode,stdout:p.stdout.toString(),stderr:p.stderr.toString(),appendOnly:after.startsWith(before),savedBytes:Buffer.byteLength(after),readback:after}));\nif(p.exitCode||!after.startsWith(before))process.exit(1);\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "fixtures/durable-04/bounded-improvement/measure.mjs",
      "originalExternalPath": "/Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-workflow-evaluation-20261016/fixtures/durable-04/bounded-improvement/measure.mjs",
      "sha256": "ced7169e2a492b4bc96fc345941929c7c18b8688f6e2b85c6d5c322eb5da6d06",
      "bytes": 2178,
      "owners": [
        "grades-native-durable-04.json#1"
      ],
      "text": "import { resolve } from 'node:path';\nconst dir=import.meta.dir;\nconst [candidate='export-base.mjs',name='baseline',rows='60000']=process.argv.slice(2);\nconst result={name,inputRevision:'ba7d1f5b02349fbac46954023c1ac94bd677aba2',configuration:await Bun.file(dir+'/freeze.json').json(),rows:Number(rows),baseline:[],candidate:[],checks:[]};\nconst sha=async p=>new Bun.CryptoHasher('sha256').update(await Bun.file(p).arrayBuffer()).digest('hex');\nresult.workloadSha256=await sha(dir+'/orders-'+rows+'.json');\nresult.baselineSourceSha256=await sha(dir+'/export-base.mjs');result.candidateSourceSha256=await sha(resolve(dir,candidate));\nfor(let i=-1;i<5;i++) for(const side of ['baseline','candidate']){\n const file=side==='baseline'?'export-base.mjs':candidate;\n const output=dir+'/output-'+name+'-'+side+'.csv';\n const p=Bun.spawnSync(['bun','--no-install',dir+'/export-cli.mjs',resolve(dir,file),dir+'/orders-'+rows+'.json',output],{env:{...process.env,NODE_ENV:'production'}});\n let sample; try {sample=JSON.parse(p.stdout.toString())}catch{sample={error:p.stderr.toString()}};\n const correct=p.exitCode===0 && Buffer.compare(Buffer.from(await Bun.file(output).arrayBuffer()),Buffer.from(await Bun.file(dir+'/expected-'+rows+'.csv').arrayBuffer()))===0 && sample.completedRows===Number(rows) && sample.errors===0;\n result.checks.push({side,index:i,exitCode:p.exitCode,stdout:p.stdout.toString(),stderr:p.stderr.toString(),correct});\n if(i>=0)result[side].push(sample);\n}\nconst stats=a=>{const times=a.map(x=>x.milliseconds).sort((a,b)=>a-b);return {median:times[2],min:times[0],max:times[4],range:times[4]-times[0]}};\nresult.stats={baseline:stats(result.baseline),candidate:stats(result.candidate)};\nconst a=result.stats.baseline,b=result.stats.candidate;\nresult.deltaPercent=100*(a.median-b.median)/a.median;\nresult.gapBeyondRange=Math.abs(a.median-b.median)>Math.max(a.range,b.range);\nresult.green=result.checks.every(x=>x.correct);\nawait Bun.write(dir+'/'+name+'.json',JSON.stringify(result,null,2));\nconsole.log(JSON.stringify({name,stats:result.stats,deltaPercent:result.deltaPercent,gapBeyondRange:result.gapBeyondRange,green:result.green,artifact:dir+'/'+name+'.json'}));\n"
    },

    {
      "path": "fixtures/durable-04/bounded-improvement/playbook-todo.md",
      "originalExternalPath": "/Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-workflow-evaluation-20261016/fixtures/durable-04/bounded-improvement/playbook-todo.md",
      "sha256": "f55d42bf0119c6289440530915c1921124d3c9242f4f5c545daab2176861506e",
      "bytes": 8191,
      "owners": [
        "grades-native-durable-04.json#1"
      ],
      "text": "# Verbatim hillclimb steps\n\n### Hillclimb\n\nYou own the metric and the experiment's integrity. Supervise and review. Delegate the attempts. For sustained, iterative improvement of one measurable thing against a target. A one-off fix is Bug fix or Perf issue. This is the loop.\n\nRead the complete [poteto mode](../SKILL.md), applied leaves and their referenced contracts. Use [session capabilities](../../../references/host-capabilities.md), [model roles](../../../references/model-roles.md), the [assignment contract](../../../references/orchestration-contract.md) and full [poteto role prompt](../../../references/agent-prompts/poteto-agent.md). Preserve the active workflow owner. Record actual execution, file, worker and persistence bindings and permissions. Missing required reads stop dependent work. Do not install companions or set up scheduling implicitly.\n\nCore discipline is one change, one measurement, keep or revert. Never stack untested changes, and never claim a win from code inspection. Apply [prove-it-works](../../principle-prove-it-works/SKILL.md).\n\n1. Ground the workload and architecture before choosing the metric. Run the complete [how](../../how/SKILL.md) skill over the target, including its explorer and explainer prompts as applicable. Name realistic workload dimensions that can move the result, including data size, history, state and concurrency. Select a case that reproduces the user's complaint. If none reproduces it, fix the repro instead of hillclimbing. Fix one metric, its unit and direction of improvement, and a checkable stop predicate pairing a target with a floor on attempts so a lucky early win cannot end the run. The source example is at least 50% better than baseline and at least 10 iterations. Use the user's numbers when given, otherwise agree them before the loop.\n2. Build the measurement harness, prove its sensitivity, then freeze it. Apply [build-the-lever](../../principle-build-the-lever/SKILL.md). Run contrasting realistic workloads and confirm the target reproduces the symptom while easier cases separate as expected. If it cannot distinguish them, revise the workload or metric. Vet it with the complete [benchmark-checklist](../../benchmark-checklist/SKILL.md) and [explain-the-number](../../principle-explain-the-number/SKILL.md) before freezing. Print error counts and completed-work counts. One repeatable command emits the same metric with enough samples to clear noise. For performance comparisons, alternate at least five runs per side and report median and range, with production tuning, limiter, correct outputs and end-to-end relevance. Record exact revisions, workload and configuration with each run. Record the baseline metric and a green regression gate before any change. Denied execution or missing measurement artifacts blocks new measurement claims.\n3. Open the decision log via the complete [show-me-your-work](../../show-me-your-work/SKILL.md) skill and its [log helper](../../show-me-your-work/scripts/log.sh). Keep the canonical trail at an explicit owned, gitignored `decision.tsv` path outside committed changes. Use that skill's TSV format and append-only helper, not a second schema. One row per attempt records the source's id, hypothesis, change, before, after, delta, tests, kept or reverted verdict and note through its decision, why, evidence and result cells. Link an owned per-attempt artifact when the details do not fit single-line cells. Read the trail before each attempt and follow its pickup, transcript audit and cross-model review rules. Verify saved records by readback. Unavailable persistence or helper execution stays a visible block, not a claimed log.\n4. Ground each hypothesis in step 1's architecture model so it names a specific mechanism, such as deferring work off the boot path because it blocks first paint. For a perf metric, order hypotheses by step 2's performance mantras in [Perf issue](perf-issue.md). Borrow only their order, not that step's stop rule.\n5. Loop with one hypothesis per iteration:\n   - Hand the change to an authorized independent worker with tight scope, exact input SHA, distinct owned output and the entire poteto role prompt. Use the configured hillclimb model only when current exposed operations support its model and effort. Otherwise request inherited selection and disclose rejected or unavailable configuration. Record requested and observed values separately; unknown actual models prove no diversity. An unavailable exact required selection blocks dispatch. Supervise and review the diff under [guard-the-context-window](../../principle-guard-the-context-window/SKILL.md). Several independent hypotheses may run in parallel only with authorized workers and separate worktrees under [separate-before-serializing-shared-state](../../principle-separate-before-serializing-shared-state/SKILL.md). Measure each isolated candidate against the same frozen harness and base. Integrate only reviewed, verified changes. Without workers, authorized sequential attempts are reduced capability and leave independent delegation unsatisfied.\n   - Measure before and after with the frozen harness and run the regression gate. Keep the workload, metric and configuration comparable. Reject an inconclusive, error-producing or skipped-work result. A gap smaller than variation is no measurable difference.\n   - Accept only when the metric moves past noise and the gate stays green. Otherwise revert the attempt's change in full while preserving unrelated work. A tweak that might help is not kept. Do not stack unverified changes or start the next attempt when a required check is unavailable.\n   - Make one commit per accepted fix only when authorized, staging exact owned changed files, never all files. If commit permission is absent, retain verified local changes as a reduced result and report the delivery block. Log every attempt, kept or reverted, with its evidence.\n\n   Each iteration ends in a check before the next begins under [sequence-verifiable-units](../../principle-sequence-verifiable-units/SKILL.md). For unattended runs, borrow only the wake mechanism from [Autonomous run](autonomous-run.md), never its stop rule. Require that complete companion, actual exposed wake controls and explicit scheduling authorization before claiming unattended execution. Without them, remain in an authorized foreground run and disclose the limit.\n6. Push past the first plateau. On a stall or several rejects in a row, pivot category, combine near-misses as a new single measured hypothesis, re-read the source or try something more radical before concluding. Correctness and simplicity outrank the number. Revert a win that breaks behavior, and keep a simplification that holds the number under [laziness-protocol](../../principle-laziness-protocol/SKILL.md).\n7. Stop when the predicate is met, or when remaining ideas are marginal and not worth their cost. Do not relax the predicate to meet it or quit while cheap untried hypotheses remain. If stuck, surface it instead of spinning. Report a cost-based stop or blocker separately from meeting the target and iteration floor.\n8. Run the complete [Opening a PR](opening-a-pr.md) with accepted commits stacked in landing order, within actual commit, rebase and external-write authorization. Missing delivery dependencies stop delivery-dependent work. Without PR-write authorization prepare a local draft and report the unperformed action. Opening a PR does not start Babysit.\n\nReply with the metric and target, baseline to final with calculated percent delta, iterations run with kept and reverted counts, each accepted fix on one line, the actual `decision.tsv` path, and the best next idea if pushed further. Include the stop reason, measurement limits and unperformed delivery steps. A zero or invalid baseline cannot support a percent claim. Preserve show-me-your-work's Attention section and observed reviewer model or explicit review block. Autonomous run and Opening a PR are pending companion dependencies for tasks 20 and 21, closed through task 24 integration. Structural checks prove no host execution or improvement.\n\nDelivery step skipped because commits and external writes are prohibited.\n"
    },

      "path": "fixtures/durable-04/bounded-improvement/prepare.mjs",
      "originalExternalPath": "/Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-workflow-evaluation-20261016/fixtures/durable-04/bounded-improvement/prepare.mjs",
      "sha256": "c8e7e8ff5b9d013a9f591c2d9270e446a2a6f243eddc20e183d77392ea772fac",
      "bytes": 1735,
      "owners": [
        "grades-native-durable-04.json#1"

      "text": "import { resolve } from 'node:path';\nconst dir = import.meta.dir;\nconst p = Bun.spawnSync(['git','show','ba7d1f5b02349fbac46954023c1ac94bd677aba2:committed-inputs/orders.json'], {cwd:resolve(dir,'..')});\nif(p.exitCode) throw Error('input revision read failed');\nconst originals=JSON.parse(p.stdout.toString());\nif (p.stdout.toString() !== await Bun.file(dir+'/input-orders.json').text()) throw Error('copied input drift');\nfor(const count of [300,60000]){\n const rows=Array.from({length:count},(_,i)=>({...originals[i%3],id:i+1}));\n await Bun.write(dir+'/orders-'+count+'.json',JSON.stringify(rows));\n const expected='id,customer,total\\n'+rows.map((r,i)=>`${i+1},${i%3===0?'\"Ada, Inc.\"':i%3===1?'Hidden':'\"Say \"\"Hi\"\"\"'},${i%3===0?'12.5':i%3===1?'4':'0'}`).join('\\n');\n await Bun.write(dir+'/expected-'+count+'.csv',expected);\n}\nconst config={inputRevision:'ba7d1f5b02349fbac46954023c1ac94bd677aba2',metric:'median end-to-end export milliseconds',direction:'lower',targetPercentReduction:50,minimumAttempts:10,workloadRows:60000,concurrency:1,environment:{NODE_ENV:'production'},warmup:1,samplesPerSide:5,cache:'warm filesystem, fresh Bun process per sample',timedRegion:'input file read and JSON parse through awaited CSV write; excludes process startup/module load',claim:'60k-row local CSV export latency is at least 50% lower with correct CSV bytes',stop:'target reduction >=50% AND measured attempts >=10, otherwise explicit blocker or marginal-cost stop',gitRevisionPolicy:'no new commits; input SHA plus immutable content SHA256 identifies prepared baseline/treatments'};\nawait Bun.write(dir+'/freeze.json',JSON.stringify(config,null,2));\nawait Bun.write(dir+'/.gitignore','decision.tsv\\n');\nconsole.log(JSON.stringify(config));\n"
    },

2143362a3126b36ea6c15596b28a6e3a3235390c73cf4398a3\\\"},{\\\"path\\\":\\\"/Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-host-verification-20261016-f462363/runtime/plugins/cache/pstack-frozen-verification/pstack-openai/0.1.0/skills/principle-laziness-protocol/SKILL.md\\\",\\\"freeze\\\":\\\"f4623636a287bea2aa79c4b2234a53d20a5f193c\\\",\\\"exitCode\\\":0,\\\"identical\\\":true,\\\"sha256\\\":\\\"0c5116c90901bb8efc6e7b44f170bc9b7dd1a55b6cb2fa9f3abfd600d5c905fe\\\"},{\\\"path\\\":\\\"/Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-host-verification-20261016-f462363/runtime/plugins/cache/pstack-frozen-verification/pstack-openai/0.1.0/skills/poteto-mode/playbooks/perf-issue.md\\\",\\\"freeze\\\":\\\"f4623636a287bea2aa79c4b2234a53d20a5f193c\\\",\\\"exitCode\\\":0,\\\"identical\\\":true,\\\"sha256\\\":\\\"d4e8519780ffeb2f0dea45b0c2fa92051c780666eba074f0033eef99875c8536\\\"},{\\\"path\\\":\\\"/Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-host-verification-20261016-f462363/runtime/plugins/cache/pstack-frozen-verification/pstack-openai/0.1.0/skills/poteto-mode/playbooks/autonomous-run.md\\\",\\\"freeze\\\":\\\"f4623636a287bea2aa79c4b2234a53d20a5f193c\\\",\\\"exitCode\\\":0,\\\"identical\\\":true,\\\"sha256\\\":\\\"d7a61255568f81fb8facddc8d8f5aaa710ce0407569175eccdf7dd050744ba1a\\\"},{\\\"path\\\":\\\"/Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-host-verification-20261016-f462363/runtime/plugins/cache/pstack-frozen-verification/pstack-openai/0.1.0/skills/poteto-mode/playbooks/opening-a-pr.md\\\",\\\"freeze\\\":\\\"f4623636a287bea2aa79c4b2234a53d20a5f193c\\\",\\\"exitCode\\\":0,\\\"identical\\\":true,\\\"sha256\\\":\\\"4198f32b22cf1d8ff367deee1dbfea805e6eecdd7ff1da3409ae7c0b07fb1c59\\\"},{\\\"path\\\":\\\"/Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-host-verification-20261016-f462363/runtime/plugins/cache/pstack-frozen-verification/pstack-openai/0.1.0/references/agent-prompts/poteto-agent.md\\\",\\\"freeze\\\":\\\"f4623636a287bea2aa79c4b2234a53d20a5f193c\\\",\\\"exitCode\\\":0,\\\"identical\\\":true,\\\"sha256\\\":\\\"9839239c6ebc72de8165f8a6fa4a6
